# Redesign v3 website to v4 with improved styling

## User

lets color-match the v3 website and fonts a bit more to our actual application  id like the code example that is used on the homepage hero to be the color palette we use for the rest of the website,m i like the font but dont love the navbar, or the uninspiring and boring install section, and i want more sexy showcases on the homepage, same with the keybindings and theme pages, they should be entierly re-done, perhaps we can base the keybinds and theme showcase on ../sema-lisp/playground but skinned in the token color palette and using the playground editor concept as a blueprint for showing code playground and themes as files, and same with ekybinds but they are shown in a different way, consult oracle and lets take a crack at it, copy over the website v3 to v4 (so we dont overwrite the old files), and lets take a crack at it, feel free to use a library  or framework to build the site (astro?).

s elem) (not (nil? (get s elem))))\\n(define (set/add s elem) (assoc s elem #t))\\n(define (set/remove s elem) (dissoc s elem))\\n\\n(define (set/union s1 s2)\\n  (foldl (fn (s e) (assoc s e #t)) s1 (keys s2)))\\n(define (set/intersection s1 s2)\\n  (foldl (fn (s e) (if (set/member? s2 e) (assoc s e #t) s)) {} (keys s1)))\\n(define (set/difference s1 s2)\\n  (foldl (fn (s e) (if (set/member? s2 e) s (assoc s e #t))) {} (keys s1)))\\n(define (set/subset? s1 s2)\\n  (foldl (fn (acc e) (and acc (set/member? s2 e))) #t (keys s1)))\\n(define (set/filter pred s)\\n  (foldl (fn (acc e) (if (pred e) (assoc acc e #t) acc)) {} (keys s)))\\n(define (set/map f s) (list->set (map f (keys s))))\\n(define (set/fold f init s) (foldl f init (keys s)))\\n(define (set/display s) (str \\\"#{\\\" (string/join (map str (set->list s)) \\\" \\\") \\\"}\\\"))\\n\\n(define (set/power-set s)\\n  (let ((elems (set->list s)))\\n    (foldl (fn (subsets elem)\\n             (append subsets (map (fn (sub) (set/add sub elem)) subsets)))\\n           (list empty-set) elems)))\\n\\n(println \\\"=== Sets ===\\\\n\\\")\\n\\n(define s1 (set 1 2 3 4 5))\\n(define s2 (set 3 4 5 6 7))\\n(define s3 (set 1 2 3))\\n\\n(println (format \\\"s1 = ~a\\\" (set/display s1)))\\n(println (format \\\"s2 = ~a\\\" (set/display s2)))\\n(println (format \\\"\\\\ns1 ∪ s2  = ~a\\\" (set/display (set/union s1 s2))))\\n(println (format \\\"s1 ∩ s2  = ~a\\\" (set/display (set/intersection s1 s2))))\\n(println (format \\\"s1 \\\\\\\\ s2  = ~a\\\" (set/display (set/difference s1 s2))))\\n(println (format \\\"\\\\ns3 ⊆ s1? ~a\\\" (set/subset? s3 s1)))\\n(println (format \\\"evens:   ~a\\\" (set/display (set/filter even? s1))))\\n(println (format \\\"doubled: ~a\\\" (set/display (set/map (fn (x) (* x 2)) s1))))\\n(println (format \\\"sum:     ~a\\\" (set/fold + 0 s1)))\\n\\n(println (format \\\"\\\\nPower set of ~a:\\\" (set/display (set 1 2 3))))\\n(for-each (fn (sub) (println (format \\\"  ~a\\\" (set/display sub))))\\n  (set/power-set (set 1 2 3)))\\n\" },\n26:     { name: \"strings.sema\", code: \";; String operations\\n(define text \\\"Hello, Sema World!\\\")\\n\\n(println \\\"Upper:\\\" (string/upper text))\\n(println \\\"Lower:\\\" (string/lower text))\\n(println \\\"Words:\\\" (string/split text \\\" \\\"))\\n(println \\\"Length:\\\" (string-length text))\\n(println \\\"Reversed:\\\" (string/reverse text))\\n(println \\\"Contains 'Sema':\\\" (string/contains? text \\\"Sema\\\"))\\n\\n;; String formatting\\n(define name \\\"Sema\\\")\\n(define version \\\"1.0.1\\\")\\n(format \\\"~a v~a — A Lisp with LLM primitives\\\" name version)\\n\" },\n27:     { name: \"text-processing.sema\", code: \";; String manipulation, regex, and text analysis\\n\\n(define sample-text\\n  \\\"The quick brown fox jumps over the lazy dog. Dogs are loyal companions.\\n   The quick brown fox ran quickly through the forest.\\\")\\n\\n;; Word frequency analysis\\n(define words (map string/lower (regex/find-all \\\"[A-Za-z]+\\\" sample-text)))\\n(println (format \\\"Total words: ~a\\\" (length words)))\\n(println (format \\\"Unique words: ~a\\\" (length (list/unique words))))\\n\\n(define freq\\n  (foldl (fn (acc word)\\n    (map/update acc (string->keyword word) (fn (v) (if (nil? v) 1 (+ v 1)))))\\n    {} words))\\n\\n(define sorted-freq\\n  (sort (map/entries freq) (fn (a b) (- (nth b 1) (nth a 1)))))\\n\\n(println \\\"\\\\nTop 10 most frequent words:\\\")\\n(for-each\\n  (fn (entry)\\n    (println (format \\\"  ~a: ~a\\\"\\n      (string/pad-right (keyword->string (first entry)) 12)\\n      (string/repeat \\\"#\\\" (nth entry 1)))))\\n  (take 10 sorted-freq))\\n\\n;; Sentence extraction\\n(define sentences\\n  (filter (fn (s) (> (string-length (string/trim s)) 0))\\n    (regex/split \\\"[.!?]+\\\" sample-text)))\\n(println (format \\\"\\\\n~a sentences found:\\\" (length sentences)))\\n(for-each (fn (s) (println (format \\\"  - ~a\\\" (string/trim s)))) sentences)\\n\\n;; Caesar cipher\\n(define (caesar-encrypt text shift)\\n  (list->string\\n    (map (fn (ch)\\n      (if (char-alphabetic? ch)\\n        (let ((base (if (char-upper-case? ch) 65 97))\\n              (code (char->integer ch)))\\n          (integer->char (+ base (math/remainder (- (+ code shift) base) 26))))\\n        ch))\\n      (string/chars text))))\\n\\n(define original \\\"Hello World\\\")\\n(define encrypted (caesar-encrypt original 3))\\n(define decrypted (caesar-encrypt encrypted 23))\\n(println (format \\\"\\\\nCaesar cipher (shift 3):\\\"))\\n(println (format \\\"  Original:  ~a\\\" original))\\n(println (format \\\"  Encrypted: ~a\\\" encrypted))\\n(println (format \\\"  Decrypted: ~a\\\" decrypted))\\n\" },\n28:     { name: \"unicode.sema\", code: \";; Unicode-aware string operations\\n\\n;; Byte length vs character length\\n(define samples (list \\\"hello\\\" \\\"héllo\\\" \\\"日本語\\\" \\\"🎉🎊\\\"))\\n(println \\\"=== Byte Length vs Character Length ===\\\")\\n(for-each\\n  (fn (s)\\n    (println (format \\\"  ~a  chars=~a  bytes=~a\\\"\\n      (string/pad-right (string-append \\\"\\\\\\\"\\\" s \\\"\\\\\\\"\\\") 12)\\n      (string-length s)\\n      (string/byte-length s))))\\n  samples)\\n\\n;; Codepoints roundtrip\\n(println \\\"\\\\n=== Codepoints ===\\\")\\n(define text \\\"Café ☕\\\")\\n(define cps (string/codepoints text))\\n(println (format \\\"  String:     ~a\\\" text))\\n(println (format \\\"  Codepoints: ~a\\\" cps))\\n(println (format \\\"  Roundtrip:  ~a\\\" (string/from-codepoints cps)))\\n\\n;; Build from known codepoints (Greek: Γεια)\\n(println (format \\\"  Greek hello: ~a\\\"\\n  (string/from-codepoints (list 915 949 953 945))))\\n\\n;; Unicode normalization\\n(println \\\"\\\\n=== Normalization ===\\\")\\n(define decomposed (string-append \\\"e\\\" \\\"\\\\u0301\\\"))\\n(define composed (string/normalize decomposed :nfc))\\n(println (format \\\"  Decomposed: ~a (length=~a)\\\" decomposed (string-length decomposed)))\\n(println (format \\\"  NFC:        ~a (length=~a)\\\" composed (string-length composed)))\\n(println (format \\\"  NFKC of ﬁ:  ~a\\\" (string/normalize \\\"\\\\uFB01\\\" :nfkc)))\\n\\n;; Case-insensitive comparison\\n(println \\\"\\\\n=== Case-Insensitive Comparison ===\\\")\\n(println (format \\\"  (string-ci=? \\\\\\\"Hello\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\"hello\\\\\\\") => ~a\\\" (string-ci=? \\\"Hello\\\" \\\"hello\\\")))\\n(println (format \\\"  (string-ci=? \\\\\\\"CAFÉ\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\"café\\\\\\\")   => ~a\\\" (string-ci=? \\\"CAFÉ\\\" \\\"café\\\")))\\n(println (format \\\"  (string-ci=? \\\\\\\"hello\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\"world\\\\\\\") => ~a\\\" (string-ci=? \\\"hello\\\" \\\"world\\\")))\\n\" }\n29:   ]},\n30:   { category: \"HTTP\", files: [\n31:     { name: \"dad-jokes.sema\", code: \";; Fetch random dad jokes from icanhazdadjoke.com\\n;;\\n;; This API returns a random joke on each request.\\n;; CORS-enabled, no API key needed.\\n\\n(println \\\"=== Dad Joke Machine 🤣 ===\\\\n\\\")\\n\\n(define (get-joke)\\n  (let ((resp (http/get \\\"https://icanhazdadjoke.com/\\\"\\n                {:headers {:Accept \\\"application/json\\\"}})))\\n    (if (= (:status resp) 200)\\n      (get (json/decode (:body resp)) :joke)\\n      \\\"(couldn't fetch a joke)\\\")))\\n\\n;; Due to the replay-cache strategy, each http/get with the\\n;; same URL+headers returns the same cached response within\\n;; one evaluation. So we get one joke per run.\\n(define joke (get-joke))\\n(println (format \\\"  ~a\\\" joke))\\n(println \\\"\\\\n  (Press Run again for a new joke!)\\\")\\n\" },\n32:     { name: \"exchange-rates.sema\", code: \";; Fetch live currency exchange rates\\n;;\\n;; Uses the ExchangeRate API (no key, CORS-enabled)\\n\\n(println \\\"=== Currency Exchange Dashboard ===\\\\n\\\")\\n\\n(define currencies (list \\\"EUR\\\" \\\"GBP\\\" \\\"JPY\\\" \\\"CHF\\\" \\\"CAD\\\" \\\"AUD\\\" \\\"NOK\\\" \\\"SEK\\\" \\\"CNY\\\" \\\"KRW\\\"))\\n\\n(let* ((resp (http/get \\\"https://open.er-api.com/v6/latest/USD\\\"))\\n       (data (json/decode (:body resp)))\\n       (rates (get data :rates)))\\n\\n  (println (format \\\"  Base: USD — ~a\\\\n\\\" (get data :time_last_update_utc)))\\n\\n  (println (format \\\"  ~a  ~a  ~a\\\"\\n    (string/pad-right \\\"Currency\\\" 10)\\n    (string/pad-right \\\"Rate\\\" 14)\\n    \\\"Inverse (per USD)\\\"))\\n  (println (format \\\"  ~a  ~a  ~a\\\"\\n    (string/repeat \\\"─\\\" 10)\\n    (string/repeat \\\"─\\\" 14)\\n    (string/repeat \\\"─\\\" 18)))\\n\\n  (for-each (fn (cur)\\n    (let* ((rate (get rates (string->keyword cur)))\\n           (inverse (if (and rate (> rate 0))\\n                      (/ (round (* (/ 1 rate) 10000)) 10000)\\n                      0))\\n           (rate-str (str rate)))\\n      (println (format \\\"  ~a  ~a  ~a\\\"\\n        (string/pad-right cur 10)\\n        (string/pad-right rate-str 14)\\n        inverse))))\\n    currencies))\\n\" },\n33:     { name: \"fetch-basics.sema\", code: \";; HTTP basics — fetching data from public APIs\\n;;\\n;; Note: CORS restrictions apply in the browser.\\n;; These examples use APIs that allow cross-origin requests.\\n\\n(println \\\"=== HTTP in the Playground ===\\\\n\\\")\\n\\n;; Simple GET request\\n(define resp (http/get \\\"https://httpbin.org/get\\\"))\\n(println (format \\\"Status: ~a\\\" (:status resp)))\\n(println (format \\\"Body is string? ~a\\\" (string? (:body resp))))\\n(println (format \\\"Headers: ~a keys\\\" (length (keys (:headers resp)))))\\n\\n;; Parse the JSON body\\n(define data (json/decode (:body resp)))\\n(println (format \\\"\\\\nOrigin IP: ~a\\\" (get data :origin)))\\n(println (format \\\"URL: ~a\\\" (get data :url)))\\n\\n;; POST with a JSON body\\n(println \\\"\\\\n--- POST Request ---\\\\n\\\")\\n(define post-resp\\n  (http/post \\\"https://httpbin.org/post\\\" {:name \\\"Sema\\\" :version \\\"1.5\\\"}))\\n(println (format \\\"Status: ~a\\\" (:status post-resp)))\\n(define post-data (json/decode (:body post-resp)))\\n(println (format \\\"Echoed JSON: ~a\\\" (get post-data :data)))\\n\\n;; ```
## 1) TL;DR
Build v4 as an Astro site with a **Token-themed design system** derived directly from `dark.yaml`, and reuse a single **“IDE/Playground” layout component** (sidebar explorer + editor pane + output pane + status bar) for both **Keybindings** and **Themes**. Keep interactivity lightweight via Astro islands: the “editor” content is mostly pre-rendered markup styled by CSS variables, and selection just swaps state + CSS vars.

Effort: **L (1–2 days)** for the full structure + homepage redesign + two interactive playground pages (assuming assets like screenshots/video exist).

---

## 2) Recommended approach (simple path)

### A. Astro project structure (in `/website/v4/`)
Use a conventional Astro layout with “design tokens + UI primitives + feature components + islands”.

```
v4/
  astro.config.mjs
  package.json
  public/
    icon.png
    screenshots/
    demos/               # mp4/webm/gif (multi-cursor, split, csv)
  src/
    pages/
      index.astro
      keybindings.astro
      themes.astro
      install.astro      # optional: or keep install section on homepage only
    layouts/
      SiteLayout.astro
      PlaygroundLayout.astro
    components/
      nav/
        Navbar.astro
        NavLink.astro
      ui/
        Button.astro
        Card.astro
        Kbd.astro
        Tabs.astro
        CopyButton.tsx    # island (or .tsx/.jsx)
        CodeBlock.astro
        EditorMock.astro  # reusable “mini editor” frame
        TerminalBlock.astro
      home/
        Hero.astro
        StatsBar.astro
        ShowcaseGrid.astro
        FeatureShowcase.astro
        InstallShowcase.astro
    islands/
      playground/
        Explorer.tsx
        KeybindingsPlayground.tsx
        ThemesPlayground.tsx
        StatusBar.tsx
      install/
        InstallTabs.tsx
    content/
      keybindings.json     # normalized data (generated or maintained)
      themes/
        dark.yaml
        github-dark.yaml
        ...
      demos.ts             # metadata for homepage showcases
    styles/
      tokens.css
      global.css
      components.css
      playground.css
    lib/
      theme.ts             # loads/parses YAML -> tokens
      shiki.ts             # optional: build-time highlighting only
      keybindings.ts       # data normalization utilities
```

Key decision: **one CSS token file** (`tokens.css`) becomes the single source of truth for colors used across the site, derived from `dark.yaml`.

---

### B. Design system: extract `dark.yaml` into CSS variables
Create `src/styles/tokens.css` that maps theme values to semantic tokens.

**Base UI tokens (from `ui.*`)**
```css
:root {
  /* Editor UI surfaces */
  --bg: #1E1E1E;
  --fg: #D4D4D4;
  --bg-line: #2A2A2A;
  --cursor: #FCE146;
  --selection: #264F78;

  --gutter-fg: #858585;
  --gutter-fg-active: #C6C6C6;
  --border: #313438;

  --panel: #252526;            /* sidebar/tabbar */
  --panel-fg: #CCCCCC;
  --panel-border: #3C3C3C;

  --overlay-bg: #2B2D30;
  --overlay-fg: #E0E0E0;
  --overlay-border: #43454A;

  --status-bg: #007ACC;
  --status-fg: #FFFFFF;

  /* Syntax palette = site accents */
  --acc-kw: #C586C0;       /* purple */
  --acc-fn: #DCDCAA;       /* yellow */
  --acc-str: #CE9178;      /* orange */
  --acc-cmt: #6A9955;      /* green */
  --acc-type: #4EC9B0;     /* teal */
  --acc-var: #9CDCFE;      /* blue */

  /* Common derived tokens (avoid inventing new colors) */
  --muted: color-mix(in srgb, var(--fg) 55%, var(--bg));
  --muted-2: color-mix(in srgb, var(--fg) 35%, var(--bg));
  --shadow: rgba(0,0,0,0.55);

  /* Typography */
  --font-sans: "Space Grotesk", system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif;
  --font-mono: "Space Mono", ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, Monaco, Consolas, "Liberation Mono", monospace;

  /* Radii + spacing (non-color tokens are fine) */
  --r-sm: 6px;
  --r-md: 10px;
  --r-lg: 14px;
}
```

**How to ensure “no arbitrary colors”**
- Only use:
  1) the literal theme colors, or
  2) `color-mix()` between existing theme colors for muted text / subtle backgrounds / borders.
- Avoid adding new hex values besides what you already have in `dark.yaml`.

**Site accent strategy**
- Treat syntax colors as branded accents:
  - Primary CTA: `--acc-var` or `--status-bg` (blue).
  - Secondary highlights: `--acc-kw`, `--acc-fn`, etc.
  - Success/warn/error can map to `overlay.highlight / warning / error` already present in YAML.

---

### C. Navbar redesign (sleeker, editor-like)
Replace the “plain links” with an editor-inspired top bar:
- Height ~48px like IDE chrome
- Left: Token logotype + blinking cursor block using `--cursor`
- Center: “tab” style navigation (`Home`, `Keybindings`, `Themes`, `Install`, `GitHub`)
- Right: primary CTA “Download” + GitHub link

Implementation:
- `Navbar.astro` fixed, with **blurred translucent background** using existing theme colors:
  - background: `color-mix(in srgb, var(--bg) 85%, transparent)`
  - border-bottom: `1px solid var(--border)`
- Active page indicator as **tab underline** using `--acc-kw` or `--acc-var` (choose one and stick to it).

---

### D. Homepage: more “sexy” showcases + better install
#### 1) Hero
Keep the hero split layout, but make it feel *like the editor*:
- Right side: not a “terminal”, but an **EditorMock** component:
  - tab bar (`--panel`, `--panel-border`)
  - gutter with line numbers (`--gutter-fg`)
  - code with syntax colors (`--acc-*`)
  - cursor block (`--cursor`)
  - subtle selection highlight (`--selection`)
- Left side copy stays similar, but the accent line uses syntax palette (e.g. keyword purple).

Component: `components/ui/EditorMock.astro` (pure markup + CSS).

#### 2) Showcases section (homepage)
Replace single screenshot with a **ShowcaseGrid** of 4–6 cards, each with:
- short title
- 1-line description
- embedded mp4/webm or animated image
- “badge” showing relevant capability (e.g. “Multi-cursor”, “CSV mode”, “Split view”, “Tree-sitter”)

Suggested showcases:
- Multi-cursor demo (short loop)
- Vertical split + two files
- CSV mode with selection + header
- Command palette / fuzzy find
- Theme switching

Implementation:
- `content/demos.ts` provides metadata.
- `ShowcaseGrid.astro` renders a responsive grid.
- Use EditorMock frames around videos to keep consistent chrome.

#### 3) Install section (make it inspiring)
Instead of boring cards + tabs:
- Present installation as **“choose your path”** in an IDE-like panel:
  - Left column: platform “files” (macOS arm/intel, Linux, Windows, Source) as explorer entries.
  - Middle: “install.sh” / “install.ps1” file content shown in editor pane.
  - Right: “Output” pane showing what happens (“Downloading…”, “Extracting…”, “Done.”)
  - Bottom: status bar: architecture + version.

This makes install feel like a miniature Token experience and reuses your playground layout components.

Implementation:
- `InstallShowcase.astro` for layout
- `InstallTabs.tsx` island for selection + copy action
- Keep commands in structured data:
  ```ts
  type InstallTarget = { id, label, fileName, command, outputLines[] }
  ```

---

### E. Keybindings page: “sema-lisp playground” blueprint
Page = `PlaygroundLayout` + `KeybindingsPlayground` island.

**Layout (3-pane + status bar)**
- Sidebar: Explorer tree
  - categories are folders: `Editing/`, `Navigation/`, `MultiCursor/`, `Files/`, `Workspace/`
  - each binding (or subcategory) is a “file” (e.g. `duplicate-line.md`, `select-next-match.md`)
- Editor pane: shows the selected “file” as content
  - top header includes a search box (“Filter…”) and maybe “Copy YAML snippet”
- Output pane: shows structured details
  - command name
  - contexts (`when`)
  - platform overrides
  - conflicts / notes

**Data model (keep simple)**
Maintain a single normalized JSON:
```json
{
  "categories": [
    {
      "id": "editing",
      "label": "Editing",
      "items": [
        {
          "id": "duplicate-line",
          "title": "Duplicate line",
          "keys": ["cmd+d"],
          "command": "Duplicate",
          "when": ["no_selection"],
          "notes": "..."
        }
      ]
    }
  ]
}
```

**Rendering approach (no heavy editor)**
- The “editor pane” is a styled `<pre>` with Token syntax coloring classes, not a real code editor.
- Show a YAML snippet for the binding, e.g.
  ```yaml
  - key: "cmd+d"
    command: Duplicate
    when: ["no_selection"]
  ```
- Output pane renders nice “inspector” rows.

Component reuse:
- `PlaygroundLayout.astro` (shell)
- `Explorer.tsx` (tree + selection)
- `InspectorPanel.tsx` (reusable for keybindings and themes)
- `StatusBar.tsx` (shows “Keybindings / Editing / duplicate-line.yaml”)

---

### F. Themes page: same playground layout, but selecting a theme changes CSS vars
Themes page = `PlaygroundLayout` + `ThemesPlayground` island.

**Sidebar**
- Theme files displayed as explorer entries:
  - `built-in/dark.yaml`
  - `built-in/github-dark.yaml`
  - `built-in/fleet-dark.yaml`
  - `built-in/github-light.yaml`

**Editor pane**
- Show the theme YAML (read-only) with syntax highlighting (optional)
- Or show a “preview source file” (Rust/TS) that stays constant

**Output pane**
- Show:
  - theme metadata (name, author, description)
  - color swatches (UI + syntax)
  - “Copy theme file name” / “Copy path” actions

**Preview**
- The main win: a **mini editor preview** that updates when selecting theme.
- Implementation trick: do not re-highlight dynamically.
  - Pre-render code markup with token classes: `.tok-kw`, `.tok-fn`, `.tok-str`, `.tok-cmt`, etc.
  - When theme changes, update CSS variables (per theme) and the preview instantly matches.

Where do theme values come from?
- Simplest: store built-in themes as JSON alongside YAML (or parse YAML at build time).
- Use `yaml` parsing at build/build-time to avoid manual duplication:
  - `lib/theme.ts` loads YAML files (Astro can import raw text) and parses them into objects.
  - Expose `ThemeTokens` to the island.

---

## 3) Rationale and trade-offs
- **Reusing the playground shell** across Keybindings/Themes/Install keeps the site cohesive and strongly “Token-like” with minimal design churn.
- **CSS variables + pre-tokenized markup** avoids shipping a heavy in-browser highlighter while still delivering the IDE aesthetic.
- Using `color-mix()` gives you necessary UI nuance (muted text, hover states) **without inventing new colors**.

Why not a real editor (Monaco/CodeMirror)?
- Overkill and risks breaking the “minimal editor” brand. Also increases bundle size and complexity.

---

## 4) Risks and guardrails
- **Color contrast pitfalls** when mixing: enforce a small set of derived tokens (`--muted`, `--muted-2`) and reuse everywhere; don’t ad-hoc mix per component.
- **Too much client-side JS**: keep islands small:
  - Keybindings selection + search only
  - Theme selection only
  - Copy-to-clipboard only
- **Responsive complexity** for 3-pane layouts:
  - At <768px: collapse into stacked panes or turn output into a tab (“Editor | Output”).
  - Keep sidebar collapsible.

---

## 5) When to consider the advanced path
Only upgrade beyond this plan if:
- You need **true syntax highlighting for arbitrary user input** in-browser (then consider Shiki-on-the-client or CodeMirror).
- Themes must preview **many languages dynamically** (then consider build-time Shiki pre-render per theme + language).
- You want a “try Token in the browser” experience (then you’re building a real playground product, not a marketing site).

---

## 6) Optional advanced path (brief)
- Build-time: generate static highlighted HTML for a set of sample files (Rust, TS, YAML, CSV) using Shiki with Token themes.
- Runtime: theme selection swaps the entire pre-rendered HTML block instead of relying on CSS vars alone (useful if token categories differ per highlighter).
- Add a lightweight virtualized explorer if the dataset grows large (likely unnecessary now).
```

Overlapping cursors merge automatically.</p>\n      <div class=\"feature-demo\">\n        <div class=\"demo-line\"><span class=\"ln\">12</span><span class=\"kw\">let</span> <span class=\"var\">name</span> = <span class=\"str\">\"alice\"</span>;<span class=\"cursor-block\">█</span></div>\n        <div class=\"demo-line\"><span class=\"ln\">13</span><span class=\"kw\">let</span> <span class=\"var\">name</span> = <span class=\"str\">\"bob\"</span>;<span class=\"cursor-block\">█</span></div>\n        <div class=\"demo-line\"><span class=\"ln\">14</span><span class=\"kw\">let</span> <span class=\"var\">name</span> = <span class=\"str\">\"carol\"</span>;<span class=\"cursor-block\">█</span></div>\n      </div>\n      <div class=\"feature-keys\"><kbd>⌥</kbd>+<kbd>Click</kbd> add cursor &nbsp; <kbd>⌘</kbd>+<kbd>J</kbd> select next match</div>\n    </div>\n    <div class=\"feature-card\">\n      <span class=\"feature-num\">02</span>\n      <h3>Split views</h3>\n      <p>Horizontal and vertical splits with independent viewports. Same file, different positions.</p>\n      <div class=\"split-demo\">\n        <div class=\"split-pane\"><div class=\"split-lines\"></div></div>\n        <div class=\"split-divider\"></div>\n        <div class=\"split-pane\"><div class=\"split-lines\"></div></div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n    <div class=\"feature-card\">\n      <span class=\"feature-num\">03</span>\n      <h3>Tree-sitter syntax</h3>\n      <p>Incremental parsing with real AST understanding. }\n.hero h1 .accent { color: var(--syn-function); }\n\n.hero-desc {\n  font-size: 1.1rem;\n  color: var(--fg-muted);\n  max-width: 480px;\n  margin-bottom: 48px;\n  line-height: 1.7;\n}\n\n.hero-actions {\n  display: flex;\n  gap: 24px;\n  align-items: center;\n}\n\n.btn-primary {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 10px;\n  padding: 14px 28px;\n  background: var(--accent);\n  color: var(--fg-bright);\n  font-family: var(--font-sans);\n  font-size: 0.95rem;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  border-radius: var(--r-md);\n  text-decoration: none;\n  transition: opacity 0.15s;\n}\n.btn-primary:hover { opacity: 0.85; }\n\n.btn-ghost {\n  font-size: 0.95rem;\n  color: var(--fg-muted);\n  text-decoration: none;\n  transition: color 0.15s;\n}\n.btn-ghost:hover { color: var(--fg); }\n\n.btn-outline {\n  display: inline-block;\n  padding: 12px 24px;\n  border: 1px solid var(--border-strong);\n  border-radius: var(--r-md);\n  color: var(--fg);\n  font-size: 0.9rem;\n  text-decoration: none;\n  transition: border-color 0.15s, color 0.15s;\n}\n.btn-outline:hover { border-color: var(--syn-variable); color: var(--syn-variable); }\n\n/* Editor Frame */\n.editor-frame {\n  background: var(--bg);\n  border: 1px solid var(--border-strong);\n  border-radius: var(--r-lg);\n  overflow: hidden;\n  box-shadow: 0 25px 60px -12px rgba(0,0,0,0.5), 0 0 0 1px rgba(255,255,255,0.04);\n}\n\n.editor-tabs {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  background: var(--panel-bg);\n  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);\n  padding: 0;\n  height: 36px;\n  gap: 0;\n}\n\n.editor-tabs .tab {\n  font-family: var(--font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.7rem;\n  color: var(--fg-muted);\n  padding: 0 16px;\n  height: 100%;\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  border-right: 1px solid var(--border);\n  background: none;\n  border-top: none;\n  border-bottom: none;\n  border-left: none;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  transition: color 0.1s, background 0.1s;\n}\n.editor-tabs .tab:hover { color: var(--fg); }\n.editor-tabs .tab.active {\n  color: var(--fg);\n  background: var(--bg);\n  border-bottom: 2px solid var(--syn-variable);\n}\n\n.editor-body {\n  padding: 16px 0;\n}\n\n.editor-content .line,\n.install-body .line,\n.feature-demo .demo-line {\n  font-family: var(--font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.8rem;\n  line-height: 1.7;\n  padding: 0 20px;\n  white-space: pre;\n  color: var(--fg);\n}\n\n.editor-content .line.hl {\n  background: rgba(255,255,255,0.04);\n}\n\n.ln { color: var(--fg-muted); margin-right: 16px; user-select: none; display: inline-block; width: 2ch; text-align: right; }\n.kw { color: var(--syn-keyword); }\n.fn { color: var(--syn-function); }\n.var { color: var(--syn-variable); }\n.str { color: var(--syn-string); }\n.typ { color: var(--syn-type); }\n.cmt { color: var(--syn-comment); font-style: italic; }\n.punc { color: var(--syn-punctuation); }\n.num { color: var(--syn-number); }\n.cursor-block { color: var(--cursor); animation: blink 1s steps(1) infinite; }\n\n@keyframes blink {\n  0%, 50% { opacity: 1; }\n  51%, 100% { opacity: 0; }\n}\n\n.editor-status {\n  display: flex;\n  gap: 24px;\n  padding: 0 16px;\n  height: 24px;\n  align-items: center;\n  background: var(--status-bg);\n  font-family: var(--font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.65rem;\n  color: rgba(255,255,255,0.9);\n}\n\n/* Stats */\n.stats {\n  display: flex;\n  justify-content: center;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 48px;\n  padding: 48px;\n  border-top: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);\n  background: var(--bg-deep);\n}\n\n.stat { text-align: center; }\n.feature-card.wide { grid-column: span 2; }\n\n.feature-num {\n  font-family: var(--font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.7rem;\n  color: var(--syn-string);\n  margin-bottom: 20px;\n  display: block;\n}\n\n.feature-card h3 {\n  font-size: 1.3rem;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  margin-bottom: 12px;\n  letter-spacing: -0.01em;\n}\n\n.feature-card p {\n  color: var(--fg-muted);\n  line-height: 1.6;\n  font-size: 0.9rem;\n  margin-bottom: 20px;\n}\n\n.feature-demo {\n  background: var(--bg);\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: var(--r-sm);\n  padding: 12px 0;\n  margin-top: 16px;\n}\n.feature-demo.compact { padding: 10px 16px; }\n.feature-demo.compact .demo-line { padding: 0; }\n\n.feature-keys {\n  margin-top: 16px;\n  font-family: var(--font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.75rem;\n  color: var(--fg-muted);\n}\n.feature-keys kbd {\n  display: inline-block;\n  padding: 2px 6px;\n  background: var(--bg);\n  border: 1px solid var(--border-strong);\n  border-radius: 3px;\n  color: var(--fg);\n  font-family: var(--font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.7rem;\n}\n\n/* Split demo */\n.split-demo {\n  display: flex;\n  gap: 2px;\n  background: var(--border);\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: var(--r-sm);\n  overflow: hidden;\n  height: 80px;\n  margin-top: 16px;\n}\n.split-pane {\n  flex: 1;\n  background: var(--bg);\n  padding: 8px 12px;\n}\n.split-lines {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  gap: 4px;\n}\n.split-lines::before,\n.split-lines::after,\n.split-pane .split-lines {\n  content: '';\n}\n.split-pane:first-child .split-lines::before { content: ''; display: block; height: 6px; width: 60%; background: var(--syn-keyword); opacity: 0.3; border-radius: 2px; }\n.split-pane:first-child .split-lines::after { content: ''; display: block; height: 6px; width: 80%; background: var(--syn-string); opacity: 0.3; border-radius: 2px; margin-top: 4px; }\n.split-pane:last-child .split-lines::before { content: ''; display: block; height: 6px; width: 70%; background: var(--syn-function); opacity: 0.3; border-radius: 2px; }\n.split-pane:last-child .split-lines::after { content: ''; display: block; height: 6px; width: 50%; background: var(--syn-type); opacity: 0.3; border-radius: 2px; margin-top: 4px; }\n.split-divider { width: 2px; background: var(--border-strong); }\n\n/* CSV demo */\n.csv-demo {\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: var(--r-sm);\n  overflow: hidden;\n  margin-top: 16px;\n  font-family: var(--font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.7rem;\n}\n.csv-row {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: 1fr 0.5fr 1fr;\n  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);\n}\n.csv-row:last-child { border-bottom: none; }\n.csv-row span { padding: 6px 10px; color: var(--fg); }\n.csv-header { background: var(--panel-bg); }\n.csv-header span { color: var(--fg-muted); font-weight: 600; }\n.csv-row.selected { background: rgba(38,79,120,0.3); }\n.csv-row .num { color: var(--syn-number); }\n\n/* Languages */\n.languages {\n  padding: 60px 0;\n  overflow: hidden;\n  border-top: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);\n}\n.languages h3 {\n  text-align: center;\n  font-size: 0.7rem;\n  color: var(--fg-muted);\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.15em;\n  margin-bottom: 24px;\n  font-weight: 400;\n}\n.marquee { overflow: hidden; white-space: nowrap; }\n.marquee-track {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  gap: 48px;\n  animation: marquee 30s linear infinite;\n  font-family: var(--font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.85rem;\n  color: var(--fg-muted);\n}\n@keyframes marquee {\n  0% { transform: translateX(0); }\n  100% { transform: translateX(-50%); }\n}\n\n/* Showcase */\n.showcase {\n  padding: 80px 48px;\n  max-width: 1200px;\n  margin: 0 auto;\n}\n.showcase-frame img {\n  width: 100%;\n  display: block;\n}\n\n/* Download */\n.download {\n  padding: 120px 48px;\n  max-width: 1000px;\n  margin: 0 auto;\n  text-align: center;\n}\n\n.install-editor {\n  margin: 48px 0;\n  text-align: left;\n}\n\n.install-tabs .tab { cursor: pointer; }\n\n.install-body {\n  position: relative;\n  padding: 16px 0;\n  min-height: 160px;\n}\n.install-pane { }\n.install-pane.hidden { display: none; }\n.install-body .line { font-size: 0.78rem; }\n\n.copy-btn {\n  position: absolute;\n  top: 12px;\n  right: 12px;\n  background: var(--panel-bg);\n  border: 1px solid var(--border-strong);\n  color: var(--fg-muted);\n  width: 32px;\n  height: 32px;\n  border-radius: var(--r-sm);\n  cursor: pointer;\n  font-size: 1.1rem;\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  transition: color 0.15s, border-color 0.15s;\n}\n.copy-btn:hover { color: var(--fg); border-color: var(--fg-muted); }\n\n.download-grid {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);\n  gap: 16px;\n  margin-top: 48px;\n}\n\n.dl-card {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  gap: 4px;\n  padding: 20px;\n  background: var(--panel-bg);\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: var(--r-md);\n  text-decoration: none;\n  transition: border-color 0.15s;\n}\n.dl-card:hover { border-color: var(--accent); }\n.dl-platform { font-weight: 600; font-size: 1rem; color: var(--fg); }\n.dl-arch { font-size: 0.8rem; color: var(--fg-muted); }\n.dl-ext { font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.7rem; color: var(--syn-comment); }\n\n/* Keys */\n.keys-section {\n  padding: 120px 48px;\n  max-width: 1400px;\n  margin: 0 auto;\n}\n\n.keys-columns {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);\n  gap: 32px;\n  margin-top: 48px;\n}\n\n.keys-group h4 {\n  font-size: 0.7rem;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.1em;\n  color: var(--syn-string);\n  margin-bottom: 16px;\n}\n\n.key-row {\n  display: flex;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  align-items: center;\n  padding: 8px 0;\n  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);\n  font-size: 0.85rem;\n  color: var(--fg-muted);\n}\n\n.key-combo {\n  display: flex;\n  gap: 4px;\n  align-items: center;\n}\n.key-combo kbd {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  min-width: 24px;\n  height: 24px;\n  padding: 0 6px;\n  background: var(--bg-deep);\n  border: 1px solid var(--border-strong);\n  border-radius: 4px;\n  font-family: var(--font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.7rem;\n  color: var(--fg);\n}\n\n.keys-cta {\n  text-align: center;\n  margin-top: 48px;\n}\n\n/* AI */\n.ai-section {\n  padding: 120px 48px;\n  max-width: 700px;\n  margin: 0 auto;\n  text-align: center;\n}\n.ai-badge {\n  display: inline-block;\n  padding: 6px 14px;\n  border: 1px solid var(--border-strong);\n  border-radius: 999px;\n  font-family: var(--font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.7rem;\n  color: var(--syn-comment);\n  margin-bottom: 24px;\n}\n.ai-section h2 {\n  font-size: 2rem;\n  margin-bottom: 16px;\n}\n.ai-section p {\n  color: var(--fg-muted);\n  line-height: 1.7;\n  margin-bottom: 32px;\n}\n\n/* Responsive */\n@media (max-width: 1024px) {\n  .hero { grid-template-columns: 1fr; gap: 60px; padding: 120px 32px 80px; }\n  .hero-editor { order: -1; }\n  .feature-grid { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr); }\n  .feature-card.wide { grid-column: span 2; }\n  .keys-columns { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr); }\n  .download-grid { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr); }\n}\n\n@media (max-width: 768px) {\n  .hero { padding: 100px 24px 60px; }\n  .features { padding: 80px 24px; }\n  .feature-grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }\n  .feature-card.wide { grid-column: span 1; }\n  .stats { flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 32px; }\n  .stat-div { display: none; }\n  .keys-columns { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }\n  .download-grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }\n  .showcase { padding: 40px 24px; }\n  .download { padding: 80px 24px; }\n  .keys-section { padding: 80px 24px; }\n  .ai-section { padding: 80px 24px; }\n  .section-title { font-size: 2rem; }\n  .hero-actions { flex-direction: column; align-items: flex-start; gap: 16px; }\n}\n</style>\n\n<script>\n  // Install tab switching\n  document.querySelectorAll('.install-tabs .tab').forEach(tab => {\n    tab.addEventListener('click', () => {\n      document.querySelectorAll('.install-tabs .tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));\n      document.querySelectorAll('.install-pane').forEach(p => p.classList.add('hidden'));\n      tab.classList.add('active');\n      const target = tab.getAttribute('data-target');\n      if (target) document.getElementById(target)?.classList.remove('hidden');\n    });\n  });\n\n  // Copy button\n  document.querySelectorAll('.copy-btn').forEach(btn => {\n    btn.addEventListener('click', async () => {\n      const pane = btn.parentElement?.querySelector('.install-pane:not(.hidden)');\n      if (pane) {\n        const text = Array.from(pane.querySelectorAll('.line'))\n          .map(l => {\n            const clone = l.cloneNode(true) as Element;\n            clone.querySelector('.ln')?.remove();\n            return clone.textContent?.trim();\n          })\n          .filter(l => l && !l.startsWith('#'))\n          .join('\\n');\n        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);\n        btn.textContent = '✓';\n        setTimeout(() => btn.textContent = '⎘', 1500);\n      }\n    });\n  });\n</script>\n"
}

## Assistant

All 3 pages build cleanly in under 500ms. Here's a summary of what was built:

### v4 Website — Complete

**Stack**: Astro (static site), zero client-side frameworks, vanilla JS for interactivity.

**Structure** (`website/v4/`):
```
src/
  styles/tokens.css        — Design system derived from dark.yaml
  styles/global.css         — Reset, fonts, scrollbars, grain overlay
  data/keybindings.ts       — All keybindings structured by category
  data/themes.ts            — All 4 themes with colors + YAML display
  components/Navbar.astro   — Translucent IDE-style navbar with tabs
  layouts/SiteLayout.astro  — Base layout with nav + footer
  pages/index.astro         — Homepage
  pages/keybindings.astro   — 3-pane playground keybindings reference
  pages/themes.astro        — 3-pane playground theme showcase
```

**Key improvements over v3:**
- **Color palette**: All colors extracted from `dark.yaml` — syntax colors (purple keywords, yellow functions, orange strings, green comments) are the accent palette for the whole site
- **Navbar**: Sleek editor-tab-style with backdrop blur, GitHub icon, blue Download CTA
- **Install section**: Redesigned as an editor window with tabbed platform commands, syntax-colored, with copy button
- **Feature showcases**: 5 interactive cards with mini code demos (multi-cursor, split views, tree-sitter, CSV, YAML config)
- **Keybindings page**: Full 3-pane IDE layout (sidebar explorer → YAML editor pane → detail inspector)
- **Themes page**: Full 3-pane IDE layout (theme file explorer → live code preview that swaps CSS vars → color swatch inspector)

Run `npm run dev` in `website/v4/` to preview.

- **Keybindings active files**: Golden, badges use orange

```json
{
  "path": "~/code/token-editor/website/v4/public/install.sh",
  "content": "#!/bin/sh\n# Token Editor — Install Script\n# Usage: curl -fsSL https://token-editor.com/install.sh | sh\nset -e\n\nREPO=\"HelgeSverre/token\"\nINSTALL_DIR=\"/usr/local/bin\"\n\n# ── Detect platform ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nOS=\"$(uname -s)\"\nARCH=\"$(uname -m)\"\n\ncase \"$OS\" in\n  Darwin) PLATFORM=\"macos\" ;;\n  Linux)  PLATFORM=\"linux\" ;;\n  MINGW*|MSYS*|CYGWIN*) PLATFORM=\"windows\" ;;\n  *) echo \"Error: unsupported OS: $OS\" >&2; exit 1 ;;\nesac\n\ncase \"$ARCH\" in\n  x86_64|amd64)  ARCH=\"x86_64\" ;;\n  arm64|aarch64) ARCH=\"aarch64\" ;;\n  *) echo \"Error: unsupported architecture: $ARCH\" >&2; exit 1 ;;\nesac\n\nif [ \"$PLATFORM\" = \"windows\" ]; then\n  EXT=\"zip\"\n  ASSET=\"token-${PLATFORM}-${ARCH}.${EXT}\"\nelse\n  EXT=\"tar.gz\"\n  ASSET=\"token-${PLATFORM}-${ARCH}.${EXT}\"\nfi\n\nURL=\"https://github.com/${REPO}/releases/latest/download/${ASSET}\"\n\n# ── Print banner ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\necho \"\"\necho \"  ┌─────────────────────────────────┐\"\necho \"  │  Token Editor — Install Script  │\"\necho \"  └─────────────────────────────────┘\"\necho \"\"\necho \"  Platform:  ${PLATFORM} (${ARCH})\"\necho \"  Asset:     ${ASSET}\"\necho \"  Install:   ${INSTALL_DIR}/token\"\necho \"\"\n\n# ── Download and install ─────────────────────────────────────────────────\nTMPDIR=\"$(mktemp -d)\"\ntrap 'rm -rf \"$TMPDIR\"' EXIT\n\necho \"  Downloading...\"\nif command -v curl >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n  curl -fsSL \"$URL\" -o \"$TMPDIR/$ASSET\"\nelif command -v wget >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n  wget -qO \"$TMPDIR/$ASSET\" \"$URL\"\nelse\n  echo \"Error: curl or wget required\" >&2; exit 1\nfi\n\necho \"  Extracting...\"\ncd \"$TMPDIR\"\nif [ \"$EXT\" = \"zip\" ]; then\n  unzip -q \"$ASSET\"\nelse\n  tar xzf \"$ASSET\"\nfi\n\necho \"  Installing to ${INSTALL_DIR}/token...\"\nif [ -w \"$INSTALL_DIR\" ]; then\n  mv token \"$INSTALL_DIR/token\"\nelse\n  sudo mv token \"$INSTALL_DIR/token\"\nfi\n\nchmod +x \"$INSTALL_DIR/token\"\n\necho \"\"\necho \"  ✓ Token installed successfully!\"\necho \"\"\necho \"  Run 'token <file>' to get started.\"\necho \"\"\n"
}
```

## Assistant

Clean build. Here's what changed:

- **Button font**: Added Inter (`--font-btn`) for all buttons — cleaner geometric sans that contrasts with Space Grotesk body text and Space Mono code
- **Install section**: Replaced the multi-tab editor block with a single prominent one-liner: `$ curl -fsSL token-editor.com/install.sh | sh` with a copy button. "Build from source" is now a collapsible `<details>` element below
- **`public/install.sh`**: Platform-detecting install script that auto-detects macOS (ARM/Intel), Linux, and Windows via `uname`, downloads the latest release, extracts, and installs to `/usr/local/bin`
